          Annual Report 2023 年報

C017167
       Devoted to bringing you great taste and a smile to your face
                             致力帶給您幸福的美味
Nissin Foods is committed to providing the highest quality products to customers. We do so by
continuously conducting research and product development meticulously, and always attaching
great importance to innovation, food quality and safety. We have also kept investing in people and
improving our production process and capabilities, so as to strengthen our overall competitiveness
and ensure sustainable growth.

Following our Group Philosophy - to be the Earth Food Creator, we contribute to society by
gratifying people with the pleasure of eating, and to the world by creating food, good food.


日清食品致力為顧客提供最優質的產品。為此，我們持續和嚴謹地進行研究及產品開發，對創
新、食品質量及安全高度重視，始終如一。與此同時，我們繼續投放資源在人力資源上，不斷
改善生產流程和提升生產力，從而提高整體競爭力以及確保可持續發展。

貫徹本集團「Earth Food Creator」的理念，我們通過創造優質美味的食物，為人類帶來飲食的
樂趣，藉此貢獻社會和世界。




          “Nissin Foodium” for you to discover more wonders and
                        surprises from Nissin Foods
           「日清食品體驗館」— 發掘更多樂趣和驚喜
To further enhance customer experience and connect with consumers, Nissin Foods has launched
its first-ever membership programme in Hong Kong and Mainland China. “Nissin Foodium”, a
WeChat mini program, offers a host of exclusive benefits, exciting rewards and interactive games
for members to discover and enjoy, in addition to providing the latest brand and product news. In
Mainland China, it is also an e-commerce platform. “Nissin Foodium” connects us to our
end-consumers, allowing direct communication for us to better understand consumer
preferences, behaviours and changing consumption trends.

We, at Nissin Foods, never stop learning and innovating.


為進一步提升顧客體驗以及連繫消費者，日清食品於中國內地及香港推出首個會員計劃。我們
通過「日清食品體驗館」微信小程式，除了為會員帶來最新品牌及產品資訊外，還讓會員發掘
及體驗一系列會員尊享禮遇、精彩獎賞及互動遊戲。於中國內地，它更是一個電子商務平台。
「日清食品體驗館」將我們與消費者聯繫起來，直接交流，從而讓我們更了解消費者的喜好、
行為，以及不斷變化的消費趨勢。

在日清食品，我們學而不倦，不斷創新。

PROFIT GROWTH FOR SEVEN CONSECUTIVE YEARS SINCE LISTING                    溢利自上市以來連續七年攀升
The year 2023 marked the resumption of normal economic activities          中國內地及香港於 2023 年解除防疫限制後恢愎經
in both Mainland China and Hong Kong following the uplift of               濟活動。年初雖然對經濟復甦充滿強烈希望，惟
restrictions against pandemics. Although there were strong hopes for       經濟活動逐漸減弱，經濟前景日漸不明朗，消費
economic recovery at the beginning of the year, economic activities        意欲趨向下跌。在此情況下，正如我們的創始人
gradually weakened, the economic outlook became increasingly               安藤百福先生所提倡的「食為聖職」企業理念，
uncertain, and consumer sentiment tended to decline. Against this          本集團透過提供令人享受及品嘗美食的樂趣，為
backdrop, the Group has contributed to people’s health and society         人們的健康及社會作出貢獻。
by providing the pleasure of eating and taste of food to people, in line
with our founder, Mr. Momofuku Ando’s corporate philosophy that
“food-related job is a sacred profession ( 食為聖職 )”.

株主の皆様へ
日清食品有限公司（以下「当社」）董事会（以下「董事会」）を代表し、当社および子会社（以下「グループ」）の 2023 年 12 月 31
日終了年度の年次報告書をお届けします。


上場来 7 期連続増益
2023 年は中国と香港においてパンデミックに対する規制が撤廃され、経済活動再開の年になりました。年初は経済回復へ
の期待が強かったものの、徐 々に経済活動が鈍化し、景気の先行き不透明感が増し、消費マインドが低下する傾向となり
ました。このような状況の下、当社グループは、創業者                                    安藤百福氏の『食為聖職（食の仕事は聖職である）』という企業
理念のもと、食の喜びと美味しさを通じて人 々の健康と社会に貢献してまいりました。


当社グループの売上高は、即席めんに対する消費マインドの低下、香港でパンデミックがなくなったことによる特需反動
減、更に人民元安の影響により前年同期比 5.8% の減収となりました。一方、原材料価格の下落、物流コストや販促費など
の販管費の減少により営業利益は前年比 2.2% の増益となり、営業利益率は 11.4% と前年比 0.9% ポイントの改善となりまし
た。当社株主に帰属する当期純利益は、2022 年 11 月に珠海永南食品の完全子会社化に伴う非支配持分の減少と日清ベトナ
ムの連結化に伴い 5.6% 増加しました。同利益率は 8.7% となり前年比 0.9% ポイントの改善となりました。当社グループは
上場来 7 年連続の増益となり利益成長を続けています。（注記：日清ベトナムの連結化に伴い、香港会計ガイドラインに基
づき、2022 年度の決算数字を遡及修正し前年比較しています）




                                                                                 ANNUAL REPOR T 2023   二零二三年年報   09
     CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT 董事長報告


     マーケティング
     2022 年 7、8 月に香港と中国でブランドロイヤリティ向上のために開始した会員制プログラム、Nissin Foodium（日清食品
     體驗館）は、対象商品を開始当初のカップヌードル（合味道）から各種日清の即席めんに範囲を広げ、登録会員は毎月増加
     しています。登録会員は WeChat の QR コードをスキャンしてポイントを貯め、特典景品と交換できます。当社はこのプログ
     ラムを通じて様 々な情報を集めながら新たな特典景品を作り、このプログラムへのリピートを高めたり、新商品開発につ
     なげています。また、試食活動を中国全土で再開し、1 年間で 3 万回且つ約 500 万人の試食を行い、日清ブランドや商品の
     美味しさの認知度を高めています。こうした地道なマーケティング活動が、日清ブランドの即席めんの購入のきっかけに
     つながっています。


     研究開発
     中国本土市場は、過去 10 年間で１人当たりの所得が増加し、消費の質の向上が人 々の生活に浸透してきました。加えて、
     香港、中国本土で価格、健康、栄養、食の安心安全への要求が高くなっています。開発チームはこうした状況の中で、製
     品の開発、新技術の導入、コストの最適化を行 ってきました。2023 年は、日清チキンラーメン と日本フォーミュラのカッ
     プヌードル を発売し、中国本土や香港の消費者の皆様に新たな食の発見と楽しみを届けました。世界初の即席めんである
     日清チキンラーメン と世界初のカップめんである日本フォーミュラのカップヌードル の味を当社中国の工場で見事に再現
     し、消費者の皆様から好評を得ています。更に、フードテックを駆使しイノベーションを進め、植物性の代替肉の開発に
     挑戦しました。一部の動物性由来の資材を原材料に使用することは、一部の消費国の規制に抵触し製品の輸入や販売が許
     可されずビジネス機会損失につながります。植物性の代替肉は自然環境にやさしく、消費者の健康志向の高まりの中、今
     後需要が増加すると予測しています。こうした R&D 活動を経験豊富な専門家集団で構成する開発チームがリードしている
     ことは、明らかな当社の競争優位性の一つとなっています。


     生産設備投資
     香港大埔工業園区に複数ある即席めん製造ラインを同じ工業園区の 1 か所の工場に移設し、自動化機器と各種 IT 装備の導
     入によりスマートファクトリー化を進め、省人化、作業安全性や製品品質の均質化の向上を行いました（2021 年 5 月概要
     公表）。また、生産を集約化することにより効率化し、生産能力を増強し、コスト削減を進めていきます。


     事業投資
     2023 年 6 月 29 日に日清ベトナムの持分約 66％を取得し連結子会社化しました。ビジネス範囲が中華圏からベトナムを含め
     た範囲に拡大しています。この日清ベトナムの子会社化により、香港・中国地区で培 った販売、管理、製造の人材やノウ
     ハウをベトナムに展開し、ベトナム市場の販売シェアを拡大します。加えて、2023 年 12 月から台湾に当社 100％出資の販
     売子会社を設立し、台湾の当社製品の販売を強化していきます。従来台湾の地場卸店経由で当社ビジネスの拡大を行 って
     きましたが、販売増に伴い販売拠点を設立し、即席めんと非即席めん製品の更なる販売を拡大していきます。


     サステイナビリティ
     創業者          安藤百福氏の『食創為世（食を作ることで社会に奉仕する）』という企業理念のもと、責任ある企業市民として持
     続可能な経営に取り組み、CO2 など環境問題や地域社会の持続的発展に貢献しています。更に従業員の待遇、モチベーシ
     ョンやエンゲージメントの向上、多様性を考慮し長期的な企業価値創造へ繋げています。2023 年、人的資本の強化のた
     め、企業フィロソフィーのゲームをモバイルアプリ化し、ゲーム化されたコンテンツを使いデジタル学習を通じて、イン
     タラクティブに経営理念や創業者の起業家精神の学習を推奨しました。企業理念や働く意義が共有され、会社全体が一体
     感を持つことを狙いとしています。こうした持続的経営の施策によって、MSCI 社から ESG 格付け A で評価されました。




10   N I S S I N F O O D S CO M PA N Y L I M I T E D   日清食品有限公司
2024 年の見通し
当社の取り巻くビジネス環境は、地政学的緊張が国際貿易と資本収支へ与える影響により不透明感が増すものの、香港や
中国本土の着実な経済成長により明るい兆しが見えてくると信じています。


中国事業ではカップヌードル（合味道）や高価格帯袋めんである出前一丁 、日清ラ王 、日清チキンラーメン の販売拡大を
目指します。更に、即席めんの販売数量を拡大するために、地理的な販売網の拡大や卸店の発掘を継続します。また、非
即席めん事業は、卸店事業や飲料事業の拡大に努めていきます。


香港事業は 100％北海道産小麦粉使用の出前一丁 、日清ラ王 、日清チキンラーメン 、米めんのベトナムフォーや様 々な高
価格帯袋めんの販売拡大に注力しています。冷凍食品に関して、冷凍ラーメンや冷凍点心のプレミアム品の販売の拡大に
取り組み、更に香港へのインバウンド旅行客が回復基調にありケータリング産業への販売も強化します。


2023 年度に連結化したベトナムビジネスは、自国マーケットの販売の強化のため、販売チャネルの拡大を目指します。台
湾販社は取り扱い卸店や主要小売店との取引拡大に努め、更にマーケティング活動を行いながら日清ブランドの認知度を
高めて、販売ボリュームの向上に努めます。


董事長としての決意
グループの運営と組織を効率的に発展させ、事業の成果を最大化を目指し、当社の株主や投資家を含むすべてのステーク
ホルダーに長期的な価値の創造を実現して参ります。そして、当社グループは地域社会の持続的な成長に貢献していきた
いと思います。


このような目標を念頭に置き、創業者   安藤百福氏の『食創為世（食を創 って社会に奉仕する）』という企業理念のもと、
美味しく安全で求めやすい食を通じ、消費者の生活を豊かにしていきます。




Kiyotaka Ando                       安藤清隆
Chairman                            董事長

CULTURE                                                                     文化
The Company upholds high standards of integrity, transparency,              本公司在業務中堅持誠信、透明度及問責制的高
and accountability in its business. It strives to foster a culture of       標準。本公司力求與持份者共同營造合規、良好
compliance, good corporate governance, and ethical behaviour with           企業管治及道德行為的文化氛圍，彼此建立信任
its stakeholders to build trust and credibility.                            及信譽。

“Peace will come to the world when there is enough food (食足世平)”,            日清日本的創辦人安藤百福先生所奉行的四個信
“Create foods to serve society (食創為世)”, “Eat wisely for beauty and          念「食足世平」、「食創為世」、「美健賢食」及「食
health (美健賢食)” and “Food related jobs are sacred profession (食為             為聖職」為本集團理念奠定基礎，並成為其創辦
聖職)”, the four tenets adopted by Mr. Momofuku Ando, the founder             之恆久價值觀。董事會致力於發展以其核心價值
of Nissin Japan, provide the basis for the Group’s philosophy and serve     為基礎的正面文化，為員工的操守及行為和業務
as the abiding values of its foundation. The Board is committed to          活動提供指引，並確保其貫穿本集團的願景、使
developing a positive culture that is built on its core values to provide   命、政策及業務策略中。
guidance on employees' conduct and behaviours as well as the
business activities, and to ensure they are embedded throughout the
Group's vision, mission, policies and business strategies.

Risk Management and Internal Control                                            風險管理及內部監控
The Group has formulated and adopted the risk management policy in              本集團已制定及採納風險管理政策以為識別、評
providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing significant         估及管理主要風險提供指導。主要風險管理程序
risks. The main features of the risk management processes comprise 5            包括五個核心流程：(a) 風險確定；(b) 風險評估及
core stages: (a) risk identification, (b) risk assessment and prioritisation,   其風險優先次序；(c) 風險應對；(d) 風險監控；及
(c) risk response, (d) risk monitoring and (e) risk reporting.                  (e) 風險匯報。

The appropriate risk mitigation plans are determined based on the               本集團根據以下風險應對措施釐定適當的風險減
following risk responses:                                                       緩計劃：

–     Acceptance: Risks are considered immaterial and acceptable                －   接受：根據本集團之風險接受程度，風險
      based on Group’s risk appetite, and therefore no action is                    被視為並不重大及可接受，故毋須採取任
      considered necessary.                                                         何行動。




                                                                                       ANNUAL REPOR T 2023   二零二三年年報   51
     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT 企業管治報告

     –       Reduction: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and actions           －   降低：風險被視為不能忽視，並採取監控
             such as controls have to be taken to reduce the impact and                  措施等行動以減少影響及風險承受程度至
             vulnerability to an acceptable level.                                       可接受水平。

     –       Sharing: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and the Group           －   分擔：風險被視為不能忽視，本集團本身
             itself cannot effectively reduce the risks to an acceptably low             無法自行將風險有效降至可接受的水平，
             level, therefore a portion of the risks has to be transferred to or         故須透過保險及外判等方式將部分風險轉
             shared with other parties by insurance, outsourcing, etc.                   移至或與其他各方共同分擔。

     –       Avoidance: Risks are so significant that there is no measure,           －   防範：風險被視為相當重大，本集團沒有
             both internally and externally, to reduce the risk to an                    足夠及有效的內部監管系統及外界措施可
             acceptable level or involve unreasonably high costs to reduce               將風險降至可接受水平或需不合理及高昂
             the risk to an acceptable level. Therefore, activities giving rise to       之成本將風險降至可接受水平。因此，須
             risks should be avoided.                                                    避開產生有關風險之活動。

PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                          主要風險及不明朗因素
A number of risks and uncertainties faced by the Group may affect its      本集團面臨多項可能影響其表現、業務營運或未
performance, business operations or future prospects. Some of these        來前景的風險及不明朗因素，當中部分為本集團
are inherent to the specific circumstances of the Group, and some are      特定情況固有的風險，部分來源自外部威脅或挑
from external threats or challenges. Significant risks are summarised      戰。主要風險概述如下。
below.

(i)    Food Safety Risk                                                    (i)    食物安全風險
The Group’s success depends on its branding and reputation and             本集團的成功依賴於其品牌聲譽，以及消費者的
consumers’ recognition and trust in its products. Food safety is the       認可和對產品的信心。食物安全是本集團的命
Group’s lifeblood. Food safety incidents may damage its reputation         脈。食物安全事件可以對其聲譽造成嚴重損害及
and cause a loss of consumers’ trust in its products. The profitability    令消費者對產品失去信心，從而可能影響本集團
of the Group may be impacted as a result.                                  的盈利能力。

As a food manufacturer, the Group places high emphasis on food             作為食品製造商，本集團非常重視食物安全及質
safety and quality management to ensure the health and safety of           量管理，以確保消費者的健康及安全。因此，本

SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                          股份獎勵計劃
Purpose                                                                     目的
A share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme” or the “Scheme”)             本公司於 2016 年 3 月 7 日（「採納日期」）採納股
was adopted by the Company on 7 March 2016 (the “Adoption                   份獎勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」或「該計劃」），並
Date”) and amended on 26 March 2024. The purpose of the Share               於 2024 年 3 月 26 日經修訂。股份獎勵計劃的目
Award Scheme is to recognise the contribution of certain selected           的為認可部分經選定僱員（包括但不限於本集團
employees (including but not limited to directors, executives, officers     任何成員公司的董事、行政人員、高級職員及其
and other employees, whether full-time or part-time, of any members         他僱員（全職或兼職））的貢獻，並向僱員給予
of our Group) and to provide the employees with incentives in order         獎勵，以留住彼等為本集團的持續營運及發展效
to retain them for the continual operation and development of the           力。
Group.

Eligible participants                                                       合資格參與者
Participants include any employee, including without limitation,            參與者包括任何僱員，包括但不限於本集團任何
the director (including former director), executive, officer and other      成員公司不時的董事（包括前董事）、行政人員、
employees, whether full-time or part-time, of any member of the             高級職員及其他僱員（全職或兼職）。
Group from time to time.

Term                                                                        期限
Subject to any early termination as may be determined by the Board          除非受董事會根據該計劃規則提出任何提前終止
pursuant to the Scheme Rules, the Share Award Scheme shall be valid         所限，股份獎勵計劃於採納日期起計 10 年期間有
and effective from the Adoption Date for a term of 10 years. The            效及生效。截至本年報日期，該計劃的剩餘年限
remaining life of Scheme is approximately 1 year and 11 months as at        約為 1 年 11 個月。
the date of this annual report.

Grant and acceptance                                                        授出及接受
Subject to the terms of the Scheme and the Listing Rules, the Board         除非受該計劃的條款及上市規則的規限，董事會
may, from time to time, grant awards to any eligible participant who        可不時向其認為已或將為本集團作出貢獻之任何
the Board considers to have contributed or will contribute to the           合資格參與者授出獎勵。
Group.

To satisfy the awards, the Company may transfer to the trustee the          為落實獎勵，本公司應向受託人劃撥所需資金，
necessary funds and instruct the trustee to acquire shares through on-      並指示受託人按當時市價透過場內交易收購股
market transactions at the prevailing market price.                         份。

Vesting                                                                     歸屬
The awarded shares and related income shall be vested in the selected       根據該計劃的條款和以下條件，獎勵股份及相關
employee at no cost to such selected employee on the vesting date           收入應於歸屬日期以零成本歸屬於有關經選定僱
subject to the terms of the Scheme and the following conditions:            員：

(a)    fulfilment of such vesting conditions (if any) as specified by the   (a)   達成本公司向經選定僱員發出的要約函中
       Board in the offer letter issued by the Company to the selected            董事會指定的有關歸屬條件（如有），且令
       employee up to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board;                   董事會合理滿意；

(b)    the selected employee remaining under the employment of              (b)   經選定僱員截至歸屬日期（包括當日）一直
       any member of the Group up to and including the vesting date               受僱於本集團任何成員公司，且該日並無
       and no notice of termination of employment of such selected                相關經選定僱員的離職通知；及
       employee is subsisting as at such date; and

(c)    the execution by the selected employee of the relevant               (c)   經選定僱員簽署相關文件，在規定的期限
       documents to effect the transfer of the awarded shares and the             內將獎勵股份及相關收入由受託人轉至經

ESG                                                                         ESG
The Group has policies to promote environmental protection. In              本集團有推行環保的政策。為了在其每個業務階
order to reduce environmental impacts at every stage of its business        段減低對環境的影響，本集團採納大量政策監控
activities, the Group adopts a wide range of policies to control            污染物排放，鼓勵循環再用辦公室供應品，使用
pollutant emissions, encourage recycling of office supplies, use water      節水設施，以及確保遵循中港兩地之環境相關法
saving facilities, and ensure compliance with environmental laws and        律及法規。
regulations both in Hong Kong and the Mainland China.

The ESG Report covering the same period is available together with          同 期 間 的 ESG 報 告 連 同 本 年 報 可 在 本 公 司 網 站

(the “Acquisition”) of Nissin Vietnam for the consideration of              9.5 百萬美元；及 (ii) 本公司同意於收購事項完成
approximately USD9.5 million, and (ii) the Company agreed to                後向日清越南注資 2.0 百萬美元（「注資」）。本公
contribute USD2.0 million to the capital of Nissin Vietnam after            司就收購事項及注資應付之總代價約為 11.5 百萬

     INFORMATION
     3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                      3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準
         statements
          The consolidated financial statements have been prepared             綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
          in accordance with the HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. For              會（「香港會計師公會」）所頒佈之香
          the purpose of preparation of the consolidated financial             港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準
          statements, information is considered material if such               則」）編製。就編製綜合財務報表而
          information is reasonably expected to influence decisions            言，倘有關資料合理預期會影響主要
          made by primary users. In addition, the consolidated                 用戶作出的決定，則有關資料被視為
          financial statements include applicable disclosures                  重大。此外，綜合財務報表亦包括香
          required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities            港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則
          on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the                及香港公司條例規定之適用披露資
          Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                                       料。


          The directors of the Company have, at the time of                    本公司董事於批准綜合財務報表時合
          approving the consolidated financial statements, a                   理預期，本集團有充足資源於可預見
          reasonable expectation that the Group has adequate                   未來持續經營。因此，彼等繼續採用
          resources to continue in operational existence for the               持續經營基準會計處理方法編製綜合
          foreseeable future. Thus, they continue to adopt the                 財務報表。
          going concern basis of accounting in preparing the
          consolidated financial statements.


          Merger accounting for business combination                           涉及共同控制實體之業務合併之
          involving entities under common control                              合併會計處理
          Effective on 29 June 2023, the Company entered into a                於 2023 年 6 月 29 日，本公司與 Nissin
          capital transfer agreement with Nissin Foods Asia Co.,               Foods Holdings Co., Ltd.（ 本 公 司 及
          Ltd. (the “Vendor”), a company wholly-owned by Nissin                Nissin Foods Vietnam Co., Ltd.（「 目
          Foods Holdings Co., Ltd., which is the ultimate controlling          標公司」）之最終控股股東）之全資公
          shareholder of both the Company and Nissin Foods                     司Nissin Foods Asia Co., Ltd.（「賣方」）
          Vietnam Co., Ltd., (the “Target Company”). The Target                訂立資本轉讓協議。目標公司之主要
          Company’s principal activities include manufacturing,                活動包括製造、進口及出口即食麵。
          importing, and exporting instant noodles. Prior to the               於交易前，目標公司為賣方之全資附
          transaction, the Target Company was a wholly owned                   屬公司。於交易後，賣方成為目標公
          subsidiary of the Vendor. Following the transaction, the             司之非控股股東。
          Vendor became a non-controlling shareholder of the
          Target Company.

3.   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL                       3.   綜合財務報表編製基準及重
     STATEMENTS AND MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY                                 大會計政策資料（續）
     INFORMATION (continued)
     3.1 Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                        3.1 綜合財務報表編製基準（續）
         statements (continued)
          Merger accounting for business combination                             涉及共同控制實體之業務合併之
          involving entities under common control                                合併會計處理（續）
          (continued)
          Under merger accounting, based on the guidance set                     在 合 併 會 計 處 理 下， 根 據 會 計 指 引
          out in AG 5, the financial information incorporate the                 第 5 號所載之指引，財務資料包含發
          financial statements items of the combining entities or                生共同控制合併時合併實體或業務之
          businesses in which the common control combination                     財務報表項目，猶如合併實體或業務
          occurs as if they had been combined from the date when                 自其首次受控制方控制當日起已經合
          the combining entities or businesses first came under the              併。
          control of the controlling party.

          The net assets of the combining entities or businesses                 合併實體或業務之資產淨值乃採用控
          are consolidated using the existing book values from the               制方角度之現有賬面值進行合併。在
          controlling party’s perspective. No amount is recognised in            控制方持續擁有人權益的條件下，共
          respect of goodwill or excess of acquirer’s interest in the            同控制合併時概不會就商譽或收購方
          net fair value of acquiree’s identifiable assets, liabilities          於被收購方之可識別資產、負債或或
          and contingent liabilities over cost at the time of common             然負債之公平值淨額高出成本的部分
          control combination, to the extent of the continuation                 確認任何金額。合併實體或業務之投
          of the controlling party’s interest. The adjustments to                入資本與相關投資成本抵銷之調整已
          eliminate contributed capital of the combining entities or             於綜合權益變動表中計入合併儲備。
          businesses against the related investment costs have been
          made to merger reserve in the consolidated statement of
          changes in equity.

          The consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                 過往年度之綜合損益及其他全面收入
          comprehensive income, consolidated statement of                        表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金流量
          changes in equity and consolidated statement of cash                   表已予以重列，以納入目標公司之業
          flows for the prior years have been restated to include                績，猶如收購事項已自目標公司首次
          the results of the Target Company as if the Acquisition                受共同控制當日起已經完成。2022
          had been completed since the date the Target Company                   年 1 月 1 日 及 2022 年 12 月 31 日 綜 合
          first came under the common control. The consolidated                  財務狀況已予以重列，以調整目標公
          statement of financial position as at 1 January 2022                   司 於 2022 年 1 月 1 日 及 2022 年 12 月
          and 31 December 2022 have been restated to adjust                      31日存續之資產及負債賬面值，猶如
          the carrying amounts of the assets and liabilities of the              該等實體或業務自其首次受共同控制
          Target Company which had been in existence as at 1                     當日起已經合併（財務影響見下文）。
          January 2022 and 31 December 2022 as if those entities
          or businesses were combined from the date when they
          first came under the common control (see below for the
          financial impacts).

          The directors of the Company considered that the financial             本公司董事認為目標公司的財務資料
          information of the Target Company is insignificant to the              對本集團的綜合財務報表而言並不重
          Group’s consolidated financial statements, therefore, no               大，故此並無列示於 2022 年 1 月 1 日
          opening balances as of 1 January 2022 were presented.                  的年初餘額。

              3.2 Material accounting policy information                                      3.2 重大會計政策資料
                      Basis of consolidation                                                    綜合基準
                       The consolidated financial statements incorporate                        綜合財務報表包括本公司以及由本公
                       the financial statements of the Company and entities                     司控制之實體及其附屬公司之財務報
                       controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is               表。當本公司滿足以下要素即擁有控
                       achieved when the Company:                                               制權：


                       •       has power over the investee;                                     •   對投資對象之權力；


                       •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its          •   對所參與投資對象之可變回報
                               involvement with the investee; and                                   承擔風險或擁有權利；及


                       •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •   有能力利用其權力以影響其回
                                                                                                    報。


                       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                       倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制權
                       investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are              要素有一項或以上出現變動，本集團
                       changes to one or more of the three elements of control                  會重新評估其是否對投資對象擁有控
                       listed above.                                                            制權。


                       Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Company                    本公司獲得附屬公司控制權時將附屬
                       obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when the                  公司綜合入賬，並於失去附屬公司控
                       Company loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,                   制權時終止綜合入賬。具體而言，於
                       income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed                 年內收購或出售附屬公司之收入及開
                       of during the year are included in the consolidated                      支，按自本公司獲得控制權當日起至
                       statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                      本集團不再控制附屬公司當日止，計
                       income from the date the Company gains control until the                 入綜合損益及其他全面收入表內。
                       date when the Company ceases to control the subsidiary.


                       Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive                      損益及其他全面收入之各項目乃歸屬
                       income are attributed to the owners of the Company                       於本公司擁有人及非控制性權益。附
                       and to the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive                屬公司之全面收入額總歸屬於本公司
                       income of subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the                擁有人及非控制性權益，即使此舉會
                       Company and to the non-controlling interests even if this                導致非控制性權益產生虧絀結餘。
                       results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit
                       balance.

                       businesses first came under the control of the controlling

                       consolidated using the existing book values from the                                  賬面值進行合併。共同控制合併時並

                       of common control combination.

                       under the common control, where this is a shorter period.


                       The comparative amounts in the consolidated financial                                 綜合財務報表的比較金額按猶如該等

           Foreign currencies                                                           外幣
           In preparing the financial statements of each individual                     編製個別集團實體之財務報表時，以

           functional currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are                  行之交易乃按交易日期當時之匯率確
           recognised at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the                       認。於各報告期末，以外幣列值之貨
           dates of the transactions. At the end of the reporting                       幣項目以當日之匯率重新換算。按外
           period, monetary items denominated in foreign currencies                     幣計值的按公平值列賬非貨幣項目於
           are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that date. Non-                  釐定公平值當日的現行匯率重新換

           in foreign currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing               益確認時，則有關損益的任何匯兌部
           on the date when the fair value was determined. When                         分亦於損益確認。當非貨幣項目的公
           a fair value gain or loss on a non-monetary item is                          平值損益於其他全面收入確認時，則
           recognised in profit or loss, any exchange component                         有關損益的任何匯兌部分亦於其他全
           of that gain or loss is also recognised in profit or loss.                   面收入確認。以外幣按歷史成本計算
           When a fair value gain or loss on a non-monetary item is                     之非貨幣項目不予重新換算。
           recognised in other comprehensive income, any exchange
           component of that gain or loss is also recognised in other
           comprehensive income. Non-monetary items that are
           measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency
           are not retranslated.

                       Income tax expense represents the sum of current and                      所得稅開支指即期及遞延所得稅開支

                       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit                      即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢利計
                       for the year. Taxable profit differs from profit before                   算。應課稅溢利與「除稅前溢利」不

                       or deductible in other years and items that are never                     稅收入或開支以及從毋須課稅或不能
                       taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax              扣稅之項目。本集團之即期稅項負債
                       is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                   乃根據於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已
                       substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                 頒佈之稅率計算。


                       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                       遞延稅項乃就綜合財務報表內資產及
                       between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities                    負債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所用
                       in the consolidated financial statements and the                          相應稅基之暫時性差額確認。遞延稅
                       corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                        項負債一般就所有應課稅暫時性差額
                       taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                    確認。遞延稅項資產一般會於可能出
                       recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred                現應課稅溢利以抵銷此等可扣稅之暫
                       tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                    時性差額時就所有可扣稅之暫時性差
                       temporary differences to the extent that it is probable                   額確認。倘暫時性差額源自初步確認
                       that taxable profits will be available against which those                （除業務合併外）不影響應課稅溢利
                       deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                    或會計溢利之交易之資產及負債，而
                       deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the             交易之時並無產生相同應課稅及可予
                       temporary difference arises from the initial recognition                  扣減暫時性差額，則有關遞延稅項資
                       (other than in a business combination) of assets and                      產及負債不會予以確認。此外，倘於
                       liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable             初步確認商譽後產生暫時性差額，遞
                       profit nor the accounting profit and at the time of the                   延稅項負債將不會予以遞延。
                       transaction does not give rise to equal taxable and
                       deductible temporary differences. In addition, deferred tax
                       liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference
                       arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.

                  highly liquid investments that are readily convertible                      可轉換為已知金額現金且價值
                  to a known amount of cash and which are subject                             變動風險不大的高流動投資。
                  to an insignificant risk of changes in value. Cash                          現金等價物乃為應付短期現金
                  equivalents are held for the purpose of meeting                             承擔而持有，而非用作投資或
                  short-term cash commitments rather than for                                 其他用途。
                  investment or other purposes.

                       Costs necessary to make the sale include incremental                      歸屬於銷售的增量成本和公司進行銷
                       costs directly attributable to the sale and non-incremental               售所必須產生的非增量成本。
                       costs which the Group must incur to make the sale.


                      Financial instruments                                                      金融工具
                       Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised                 當集團實體成為工具合約條文之一方
                       when a group entity becomes a party to the contractual                    時，會確認金融資產及金融負債。所
                       provisions of the instrument. All regular way purchases or                有以正規途徑購買或出售之金融資產
                       sales of financial assets are recognised and derecognised                 乃按交易日期基準確認及終止確認。
                       on a trade date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are                 以正規途徑購買或出售為要求在法規
                       purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery              或慣例所確定之時間框架內於市場交
                       of assets within the time frame established by regulation                 付資產之金融資產購買或出售。
                       or convention in the marketplace.

           The effective interest method is a method of calculating                     實際利息法為計算金融資產或金融負

           received that form an integral part of the effective interest                成實際利率組成部分的所有費用及點

           under HKFRS 9 Financial Instruments. The amount of ECL                       項及銀行結餘）進行減值評估。預期

           Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                        全期預期信貸虧損指相關工具於預計
           all possible default events over the expected life of the                    全期內所有可能違約事件所產生的預
           relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                         期 信 貸 虧 損。 相 反，12 個 月 預 期 信
           ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                         貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指
           expected to result from default events that are possible                     預計於報告日期後 12 個月內可能發
           within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment                        生的違約事件產生的部分全期預期信
           are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                         貸虧損。評估乃根據本集團過往信貸
           experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the                    虧損經驗，並就債務人特定因素、一
           debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                       般經濟狀況以及對於報告日期之當時
           of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                      狀況及未來狀況預測的評估作調整。
           well as the forecast of future conditions.

           receivables. The ECL on these assets are assessed                            預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期信貸
           individually for debtors.                                                    虧損乃就債務人進行個別評估。

           a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,             除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
           the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment of                         上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧

4.   KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                                       4.   估計不明朗因素之重要來源
     In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are                 於應用本集團之會計政策時（詳述於附註
     described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required                   3)，本公司董事須對未能透過其他來源確
     to make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the                          定之資產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及
     carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily                  假設。所作出之估計及相關假設乃以過往
     apparent from other sources. The estimates and underlying                        經驗及其他被視為相關之因素為基準。實
     assumptions are based on historical experience and other                         際業績可能與該等估計有異。
     factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results may
     differ from these estimates.


     The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                         估計及相關假計乃按持續基準審閱。倘對
     an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                         會計估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間，
     recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if the                 則於該期間確認，或倘修訂會影響目前及
     revision affects only that period, or in the period of the revision              未來期間，則會於修訂及未來期間確認。
     and future periods if the revision affects both current and future
     periods.

      35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                   35. 資本風險管理
              The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團之資本管理旨在確保本集團旗下實
              Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 體將能持續經營，同時透過經營產生之溢
              maximising the return to shareholders through the generation            利為利益相關方爭取最高回報。本集團之
              of profits from its operations. The Group’s overall strategy            整體策略較過往年度維持不變。
              remains unchanged from prior year.


              The capital structure of the Group consists of cash and cash            本集團資本結構包括現金及現金等價物及
              equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the Company,           本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及
              comprising issued share capital and reserves.                           儲備）。


              The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an         本公司董事按年度基準審閱資本結構。作
              annual basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the        為此審閱之一環，董事考慮資本成本及與
              cost of capital and the risks associated with its capital. Based on     資本相關之風險。本集團將根據董事之意
              recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance its            見透過支付股息及發行新股來平衡其整體
              overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and          資本結構。
              new share issues.
